##### `Python/pystate.c` (modified; +2 / -0)

```diff
@@ -963,6 +963,8 @@ PyInterpreterState_Delete(PyInterpreterState *interp)
 
     _PyObject_FiniState(interp);
 
+    PyConfig_Clear(&interp->config);
+
     free_interpreter(interp);
 }
 
```

##### `Python/pylifecycle.c`

**kumaraditya303**, 2025-10-18T15:37:51Z:

```diff
@@ -2505,6 +2507,7 @@ new_interpreter(PyThreadState **tstate_p,

         Py_EndInterpreter(tstate);
     } else if (interp != NULL) {
+        PyConfig_Clear(&interp->config);
```

```text
Why not call this in `PyInterpreterState_Delete` itself?
```

**ashm-dev**, 2025-10-18T15:46:46Z:

```diff
@@ -2505,6 +2507,7 @@ new_interpreter(PyThreadState **tstate_p,
     if (tstate != NULL) {
         Py_EndInterpreter(tstate);
     } else if (interp != NULL) {
+        PyConfig_Clear(&interp->config);
```

```text
Thank you for the review! If I understand correctly, you're suggesting to add `PyConfig_Clear` in `PyInterpreterState_Delete` before `free_interpreter`. 
```

**kumaraditya303**, 2025-10-18T15:50:09Z:

```diff
@@ -2505,6 +2507,7 @@ new_interpreter(PyThreadState **tstate_p,
     if (tstate != NULL) {
         Py_EndInterpreter(tstate);
     } else if (interp != NULL) {
+        PyConfig_Clear(&interp->config);
```

```text
Yes
```

**ashm-dev**, 2025-10-18T15:55:00Z:

```diff
@@ -2505,6 +2507,7 @@ new_interpreter(PyThreadState **tstate_p,
     if (tstate != NULL) {
         Py_EndInterpreter(tstate);
     } else if (interp != NULL) {
+        PyConfig_Clear(&interp->config);
```

## Source Records

Complete verbatim content of the issue body and every comment. Sanitizer stacks, shadow bytes, allocation/free histories, and reproducers are preserved exactly as posted.

### Original Issue

Source: [https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140301](https://github.com/python/cpython/issues/140301)

````text
# Bug report

### Bug description:

LeakSanitizer detects memory leaks when running `test_capi` with refleak detection. The leaks occur during subinterpreter creation via `Py_NewInterpreterFromConfig()`.

## Affected Versions

- Python 3.12
- Python 3.13  
- Python 3.14
- CPython main branch

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Build CPython with AddressSanitizer and debug mode:
```bash
CC=clang CXX=clang++ ./configure --with-address-sanitizer --with-pydebug --with-undefined-behavior-sanitizer --disable-optimizations && make -j$(nproc)
```

2. Run test_capi with refleak detection:
```bash
./python -X dev -X showrefcount -m test -R 5:5 test_capi -j$(nproc)
```

## Logs

[312.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/22984810/312.log)
[313.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/22984809/313.log)
[314.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/22984812/314.log)
[main.log](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/22984811/main.log)

### CPython versions tested on:

3.15, CPython main branch, 3.14, 3.13, 3.12

### Operating systems tested on:

Linux
````
